## Document 008: 10月收官日！沪指跌超1%失守3600点，创业板跌超4%，白酒、医药爆发，光模块“易中天”大跌，30年期国债创3月来最大跌幅

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月收官日！沪指跌超1%失守3600点，创业板跌超4%，白酒、医药爆发，光模块“易中天”大跌，30年期国债创3月来最大跌幅.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

盘⾯上，个股跌多涨少，沪深京三市超 4600 股飘绿，今⽇成交 1.69 万亿。板块⽅⾯，光通
信、传媒、软件、半导体、军⼯板块领跌，⽩酒、 CRO 、航运板块全天强势，银⾏、煤炭
板块午后⾛俏。
摘要：
A 股和港股算⼒硬件、存储芯⽚等科技股承压，光模块⻰头 “ 易中天 ” ⼤跌：此前受到⾕歌财报重
挫市场信⼼，隔夜美股遭遇抛售，叠加亚⻢逊和英特尔盘后双双⼤跌，以及韩国综合指数收盘
跌 3.53% ， SK 海⼒⼠跌超 7% 。同时新易盛和中际旭创最新财报公布显⽰，新易盛三季度净利润
环⽐下降 19% ，存货⼤增 141% ；中际旭创Q3 营收环⽐增 24.62% ，净利润环⽐增 8.46% ，控股
股东减持 32 亿。
10 ⽉ 30 ⽇， A 股各⼤指数早盘低开回升，午盘跌幅显著收窄，午后三⼤指数⼀度拉升翻红，随后
⼜再度回落，创业板和科创 50 指数跌幅显著，因算⼒硬件等科技股再度陷⼊调整，光模块、光
通信、光芯⽚等概念股集体下挫， “ 易中天 ” ⻬跌，新易盛⼤跌 14% ，天孚通信跌超 8% 。⽩酒、

饮料、⽩⾊家电等消费股反弹。创新药、 CRO 逆势拉升。银⾏、煤炭板块午后⾛俏，银⾏股批
量新⾼。传媒股⼤⾯积跌停。
多只 A500ETF 较昨⽇全天放量成交，创业板 ETF 易⽅达、科创 50ETF 华夏成交额再度双双突破
100 亿元， A500ETF 华泰柏瑞成交额 68 亿元，较昨⽇全天放量 6% 、 A500ETF 南⽅成交额 64 亿
元，较昨⽇全天放量 7% 、 A500ETF 易⽅达成交额 39 亿元，较昨⽇全天放量 30% 、 A500ETF 华
夏成交额 18 亿元，较昨⽇全天放量 61% 。
港股全天震荡上涨，恒指、恒科指集体⾛⾼，恒指涨超 1% ，恒科指冲⾼回落，盘中⼀度涨超
1.5% ，权重科⽹股多数反弹，⼩⽶、美团、阿⾥、携程、⽹易等纷纷⾛⾼。光模块等算⼒硬件
下挫，中际旭创 H 股跌超 4% ，盘初⼀度跌超 7% 创上市以来新低。存储芯⽚、 AI ⼤模型股同样承
压，智谱⼤跌 9% 。债市⽅⾯，国债期货全线下跌， 30 年期国债期货跌幅创今年 3 ⽉以来最⼤。

商品⽅⾯，国内商品期货涨跌参半。
A 股：截⾄收盘，沪指跌 1.44% ，失守 3600 点，深成指跌 2.73% ，创业板指跌 4.13% 。从 10 ⽉来
看，创业板和科创 50 指数⽉跌超 23% ，创业板指较今年 6 ⽉的历史⾼点跌幅超 44% ，深成指、中
证 500 、中证 2000 均跌超 15% ，沪指跌超 6% 。
盘⾯上，个股跌多涨少，沪深京三市超 4600 股飘绿，今⽇成交 1.69 万亿。板块⽅⾯，光通信、
传媒、软件、半导体、军⼯板块领跌，⽩酒、 CRO 、航运板块全天强势，银⾏、煤炭板块午后
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⾛俏。
从本⽉来看，红利股逆势⾛强，建设银⾏、⼯商银⾏、中国银⾏、中信银⾏、陕西煤业创历史新
⾼，中国神华续创近 19 年新⾼，中国⽯油创 18 年新⾼；物理 AI 凡拓数创股价翻倍，⼤业股份涨
超 90% 。下跌⽅⾯，算⼒硬件股本⽉跌幅居前， CPO 、 PCB ⽅向集体⾛弱，源杰科技、⻓光华
芯、⼴合科技跌超 50% ；锂电⻰头股宁德时代⽉线三连阴，⽉跌近 20% ，股价⾃ 2026 年 5 ⽉ 7 ⽇
创出的历史⾼点回落超 50% 。
港股：截⾄收盘，恒指收涨 1.46% ，恒⽣科技指数涨 0.29% 。
盘⾯上，⽯油⽯化、家电、银⾏板块⾛强。蒙⽜乳业、中⽯油、中⽯化、海尔智家领涨。⼈⼯智
能主题继续调整。

### Page 3

债市：国债期货全线下跌，截⾄收盘， 30 年期主⼒合约跌 0.94% ，创 3 ⽉ 9 ⽇以来最⼤跌幅， 10
年期主⼒合约跌 0.21% ， 5 年期主⼒合约跌 0.17% ， 2 年期主⼒合约跌 0.06% 。
商品：国内商品期货涨跌参半，截⾄收盘，农副产品涨幅居前苹果涨 6.57% ；化⼯品多数上涨，
沥⻘涨 3.49% ；⾮⾦属建材全部上涨，玻璃涨 1.53% ；能源品多数上涨， LPG 涨 1.33% ；油脂油
料多数上涨，棕榈油涨 0.90% ；贵⾦属跌幅居前，⽩银跌 6.39% ；基本⾦属多数下跌，锡跌
5.80% ；新能源材料全部下跌，多晶硅跌 3.65% ；⿊⾊系多数下跌，焦煤跌 1.94% ；航运期货全
部下跌，集运指数 ( 欧线 ) 跌 1.54% 。

### Page 4

⽩酒 / ⼤消费逆势爆发：泸州⽼窖等多股涨停
泸州⽼窖尾盘强势封板；皇台酒业、古井贡酒、迎驾贡酒三者涨停，⼭西汾酒涨超 8% ，舍得酒
业涨超 7% ，五粮液涨超 3% ，贵州茅台涨超 2% 。

### Page 5

这轮⽩酒⾏情由 " 三季报业绩改善信号  + 机构密集看多 " 两条线索驱动。酒企三季报释放业绩改善
信号，是今⽇⽩酒全线上涨的直接催化。
中信证券认为，当前⽩酒板块处于周期磨底后期、出清尾声阶段，价格、库存、预期等底部信
号已现；展望 2027 年，⽩酒⾏业供需有望实现新均衡 —— 需求有望在宏观催化和低基数下实现
弱复苏，供给预计进⼀步收缩但竞争加剧。
消费⽅向的延伸同样活跃。午后乳业板块拉升，阳光乳业、新乳业涨停，南⽅乳业涨超 10% ，
品渥⻝品、妙可蓝多、骑⼠乳业、天润乳业跟涨。；⻘岛⻝品⾛出 5 连板；泉阳泉、好想你、⿊
芝⿇涨停。

### Page 6

华泰证券研报预计，中国 B 端乳制品市场规模 2028 年将达 703 亿元、复合增⻓率 14.3% ，新式茶
饮、咖啡、烘焙等场景正成为乳品消费新引擎。
医药医疗 /CRO 集体⾛强：万邦医药 20CM 涨停，礼来业绩超预期映射
万邦医药 20CM 涨停，睿智医药午后 20CM 涨停，阳普医疗涨 20.03% ；近岸蛋⽩涨超 18% ；百花
医药涨 9.96% 、澳洋健康 3 连板、羚锐制药、桂林三⾦ 2 连板、⼤参林、九安医疗、永安药业涨
停。
催化来⾃海外映射与政策端共振。

### Page 7

华源证券指出，经过 2015—2025 年的⼗年创新转型，中国医药产业已完成新旧增⻓动能转换
—— 创新替代仿制、出海能⼒提升，尤其是创新药显著打开了中国医药企业增⻓新曲线。展望
2026 年，继续看好以创新为主的医药科技主线，重点关注创新药、脑机接⼝、 AI 医疗、⼿术机
器⼈等；同时建议布局 2026 年⾏业有望迎来反转的⽼龄化及院外消费，关注估值较低的⿇药、
⾎制品、中药等。
东⽅证券表⽰， GLP-1 药物处于需求扩容与产品迭代并⾏阶段，全球市场商业化仍有巨⼤空间，
国内放量和海外市场拓展有望共同驱动企业成⻓。
航运港⼝逆势⾛强：中远海控 AH 股⻬创历史新⾼
锦江航运涨停；中远海控 A 股涨近 7% 、 H 股涨 7.52% 报 17.87 港元， AH 股同步创出历史新⾼， A
股最新市值约 2641 亿元；国航远洋、中远海能、招商轮船、上港集团跟涨。
其⼀， 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇美股⽣物制药巨头礼来公布三季报，净利润 64.51 亿美元、同⽐增⻓ 16% ，业绩
⾼于市场预期，隔夜美股医疗保健板块⼤涨 2.5% ， CRO 与海外创新药景⽓⾼度联动，资⾦在科

技股调整之际回流医药。
其⼆，脑机接⼝⽅向政策密集催化 —— 近⽇由北京脑科学与类脑研究所承担的三项 " 脑科学与类
脑研究 " 国家科技重⼤专项项⽬启动会召开，午后脑机接⼝概念异动拉升，百洋医药、美好医疗
涨超 10% ，博济医药、爱朋医疗、创新医疗、翔宇医疗跟涨。

### Page 8

催化由运价与业绩双轮驱动。运价端， 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇⻢⼠基发布公告，宣布上调远东⾄北欧及地中
海航线运价、新费率⾃ 2026 年 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇起⽣效； 10 ⽉中旬以来，达⻜、⻢⼠基、赫伯罗特和地
中海航运已相继宣布将远东发往欧洲、地中海、北⾮的 FAK 运价上调⾄ 4200—4500 美元 /FEU 。
据航运咨询机构德鲁⾥数据，受跨太平洋与亚欧航线运价上涨推动，世界集装箱运价指数
（ WCI ）上涨 2% 、 40 英尺标准集装箱（ FEU ）运价达 4585 美元。
业绩端，中远海控 28 ⽇晚间披露三季报，前三季度营收 1876.14 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 11.94% ，归⺟
净利润 306.49 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 13.22% ；第三季度营收 756.92 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 29.39% ，归⺟净
利润 172.30 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 80.73% （平均⽇赚约 1.87 亿元）。
国泰海通证券认为，油运已进⼊⾼景⽓阶段且具备可持续性，打开业绩与估值双重空间，维持
油运 " 增持 " 评级。中信证券研报指出，有效运⼒需求螺旋上升和中东出货量增加持续挤压运输

端，随着旺季货盘后续集中释放， VLCC 运价有望再次上⾏，建议关注消息⾯错杀带来的布局机
会。
算⼒硬件 /CPO 继续重挫：新易盛跌超 14%
算⼒硬件是今⽇最⼤的 " 失⾎点 " ，也是拖累创业板指跌 4.13% 、科创 50 跌 5.48% 的直接元凶。新
易盛跌 14.19% ，胜宏科技跌 13.31% ，天孚通信跌 8.42% ，中际旭创跌 4.99% ；移远通信跌停，
振邦智能、环旭电⼦、联创电⼦跌停。

### Page 9

这⼀轮下跌由内外因素叠加。外因是隔夜美股抛售的传导 ——Alphabet 财报不及预期收跌
13% 、市值蒸发 4790 亿美元， ServiceNow 暴跌 27% ，英特尔盘后暴跌 21.6% ，市场对 AI 资本开
⽀能否回本的担忧升温，情绪直接传导⾄ A 股 CPO/ 光模块产业链。
内因是 " 易中天 " 业绩结构性问题被放⼤ —— 新易盛三季度营收、净利润已由环⽐增⻓转为环⽐下
降（分别环⽐下降 13% 和 19% ），存货⼤增；天孚通信、中际旭创虽营收环⽐增速加快，但净利
润环⽐增速放缓，市场对数据中⼼建设 " 能否回本 " 的质疑与光互连路线交织的焦虑共振，引发获
利盘集中兑现。

### Page 10

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關

民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，

     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。

受到油價及美債殖利率回落，外加法國社會局勢略見緩解影響，帶動匯價反彈至
1.1260遇到賣壓後回落，後續大致在1.1190至 1.1250間波動；臨近週末，歐元區10
月份 PMI表現低迷，且市場對於法國政局和財政赤字擔憂持續，拖累 EUR/USD轉
若，匯價一度下行來至 1.1183，終場收在 1.1202，週線下跌 37點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏空，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元區10月 PMI
顯示德、法表現分化，法國製造業及服務業均表現疲弱低於50榮枯線，拖累歐元區
10 月綜合 PMI 由 9 月 53.1 下滑至 51.9，為四個月以來新低，經濟基本面承壓將不
利 EUR/USD上行；此外，法國社會及政經局勢不穩導致法債殖利率突破5%，市場
對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。

新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.19-115.10.22

30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68%  
  公債－10年期- A15109 2.000%~2.002% 
      － 5 年期- A15108     1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公
債供給相對有限，可望抑制殖利率大幅彈升。預期短線台債殖利率維持區間整
理，5 年券及 10 年券交易區間分別估於 1.82%~1.84%及 1.98%~2.01%。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數初值由 58.4 降至 57.6，企業活動仍強勁擴
張，內需及出口帶動訂單與就業成長；惟油價、關稅及供應鏈瓶頸推升
成本，售價漲幅創 2022 年 8 月以來新高，通膨壓力持續升溫，後續須
關注聯準會政策動向。 
⚫ 9 月美國住宅市場續顯疲弱，建築許可及新屋開工分別年減 3.0%及
7.4%，反映住宅投資動能放緩。儘管多戶住宅許可與完工數回升，但整
體營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率、住房負擔能力及營建成本
對市場復甦的影響。 
⚫ 密西根大學公布之 10 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.0，月減 0.2%、年
減 10.4%，顯示民眾對目前經濟環境仍缺乏信心，雖然對未來景氣的看
法略有改善，但高物價與借貸成本仍讓消費者感到壓力。此外，未來一
年及長期通膨預期分別升至 4.7%及 3.5%，反映民眾仍擔心物價持續上
漲。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 23 日川普宣布調整加拿大商品 50%關稅清單，10 月 30 日生效，
豁免部分紙製品、機械及包裝材料，並新增地毯、油漆等課稅項目。外
媒認為此舉與期中選舉有關，有助降低部分企業成本，但美加貿易摩擦
仍未化解。 
短評 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合產出指數雖略為下滑 ， 但仍維持高檔 ， 顯示內需與就業持
續支撐企業活動擴張。然而，消費者信心仍處低檔，反映高物價與借貸
成本持續壓抑購買意願，儘管民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但新屋
開工及建築許可同步下滑，顯示消費與住宅投資前景仍面臨挑戰。另一
方面，美國調整部分加拿大商品關稅，雖有助減輕部分企業進口成本，
但雙邊貿易摩擦尚未化解，政策不確定性仍存。

法國動盪短期內難降溫，料將使央行保持審慎觀望態度

重要 
事件 
⚫ 歐洲議會代表團於 10 月 27 日至 29 日訪問中國，料將討論稀土及綠能
技術的依賴、貿易失衡，以及歐洲企業在中國面臨的不平等待遇問題。 
⚫ 法國抗爭活動規模逐漸擴大 ， 各大工會與抗議團體已宣布在 10 月 31 日
再度發起大規模遊行，並預告於 11 月 5 日舉行全國性大罷工。 
短評 
⚫ 法國政府既無法強力通過重大的財政改革 ， 也難以快速推出安撫反對派
的妥協法案或者公共支出 。 部分較為悲觀的機構認為此波動盪可能會延
續至 2027 年 4 月的法國總統大選。 
⚫ 市場預期歐洲央行在 10 月 28 日的政策會議將採取觀望立場。 
日 
本 
貿易逆差及通膨壓力延續，內需仍具韌性 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月出口年增 16.7%。 
⚫ 9 月全國消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.1%。 
⚫ 10 月綜合 PMI 持平於 52.3。 
短評 
⚫ 出口維持強勁、服務需求亦持續擴張，惟中東局勢推升能源進口成本，
使貿易逆差擴大，並加重國內通膨壓力。隨成本上漲逐步由商品擴散至
服務價格，通膨黏著性可能進一步提高，為日本央行貨幣政策正常化提
供支撐；另一方面，原物料供應受限及投入成本居高不下，已開始影響
製造業動能與企業信心，後續仍須關注高成本環境對經濟成長的制約。 
中 
國 
第三季經濟成長率回升，但前景仍較為不利 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第三季 GDP 年增率初估值為 4.5%。 
⚫ 1-9 月累計零售額年增率持平為 1.1%。 
⚫ 1-9 月固定資產投資年減幅維持於 7.2% 
⚫ 1-9 月房地產開發投資額年減幅由 19.9%擴大至 20.6%。 
⚫ 9 月工業生產年增率較 8 月增加 0.3 個百分點至 5.5%；累計 1-9 月工業
生產年增率持平 1-8 月水準為 5.3%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 歐盟計劃最快將於 2027 年首季擴大對中國產品的進口限制，涵蓋化學
品、家具及機械等產品，不再僅限電動車。

一、 美國—企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
標普全球(S&P Global)公布，2026 年 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數(Composite 
PMI Output Index) 初值由上月的 58.4 降至 57.6，其中服務業採購經理人指數
(Services PMI)由 58.8 降至 57.9，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI)則由
55.9 微升至 56.0，創 2022 年 5 月以來新高，企業活動雖略有放緩，但仍維持強
勁擴張。需求方面，內需穩健及出口改善帶動整體新訂單增速創逾 4 年半新高，
商品出口訂單亦為 2025 年 6 月以來首次增加；企業持續擴增人力，整體就業增
速為 2022 年 6 月以來次高。另一方面，未完成訂單增加、供應商交貨延遲加劇，
加上油價上漲及關稅推升能源、運輸與原物料成本，使企業投入成本維持高檔，
帶動售價漲幅升至 2022 年 8 月以來最高。儘管企業對未來一年營運前景的樂觀
程度略降，但仍處於今年相對高檔。整體而言，美國經濟在內外需及就業支撐下
維持擴張，惟供應鏈瓶頸與成本上升加劇通膨壓力，後續仍須關注物價走勢及聯
準會貨幣政策動向。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 10 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.0，略低於
上月的 48.1，月減 0.2%、年減 10.4%，並較今年 2 月下降約 15%，顯示消費者信
心持續處於低檔。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由 50.9 降至 46.7，月減 8.3%、
年減 20.3%；消費者預期指數則由 46.3 回升至 48.8，月增 5.4%、年減 3.0%，反
映民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但整體信心仍偏弱。調查指出，高利率及購
買負擔壓力使耐久財購買條件下降 18%，拖累當前經濟狀況指數；未來一年企業
景氣預期雖改善約 14%，但仍低於去年同期 16%。通膨預期方面，未來一年通膨
預期由 4.6%升至 4.7%， 長期通膨預期亦由3.4%升至 3.5%， 雙雙連續第二個月攀
升，創今年 5 月以來最高水準。密西根大學指出，2025 年關稅政策衝擊及 2026
年伊朗衝突引發的能源價格上漲，使民眾通膨預期再度升溫，且長期預期已明顯
高於 2019 年及 2024 年水準。整體而言，10 月美國消費者信心大致持平，但高物
價與借貸成本仍壓抑購買意願，加上短期及長期通膨預期持續上升，可能增加物
價回穩的難度，後續仍須關注能源價格、家庭消費及通膨預期變化對聯準會貨幣
政策的影響。

2026 年 9 月美國住宅市場建築許可年化總數降至 140.0 萬戶，月減0.2%、年
減 3.0%，其中單戶住宅建築許可降至 84.5 萬戶，月減 4.0%、年減 4.8%，惟 5 戶
以上多戶住宅建築許可增至 50.3 萬戶，月增 5.9%、年增 1.0%，顯示不同住宅類
型開發規劃表現分歧。新屋開工年化數降至 122.1 萬戶，月減5.2%、年減 7.4%，
其中單戶住宅開工 85.9 萬戶，較去年同期仍增 2.6%，但多戶住宅開工降至 34.9
萬戶，年減25.9%，拖累整體營建動能。施工方面，在建住宅維持128.0 萬戶，與
上月持平但較去年減少 3.3%；完工年化數則回升至 119.2 萬戶，月增2.2%，主要
受多戶住宅完工月增 14.9%帶動，惟整體仍較去年同期減少 14.0%。整體而言，
美國住宅市場呈現建築許可及新屋開工下滑、在建規模持平及完工略有回升的態
勢，反映住宅營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率走勢、住房負擔能力及營
建成本變化對住宅市場復甦的影響。 
美國總統川普 10 月 23 日依據《1930 年關稅法》第 338 條簽署公告，調整部
分加拿大商品加徵 50%關稅的適用範圍，並自 10 月 30 日起生效。此次調整取消
膠合板、部分紙製品、機械與冷凍設備、包裝用木箱及紙箱、新鮮與乾燥海藻等
商品的額外關稅，同時新增閥門、地毯、油漆、洗髮精、可可粉、木製棺材、空
調設備及獨木舟等課稅項目，呈現局部鬆綁與新增課稅並行的政策方向。白宮表
示 ， 此舉旨在減輕加拿大貿易措施對美國企業造成的負擔 ， 並維護國內產業利益 。
由於緬因州農業及製造業高度依賴加拿大進口物資，共和黨參議員柯林斯 (Susan 
Collins)積極向白宮爭取關稅豁免 ， 外媒亦將此次調整與11 月 3 日期中選舉連結 。
整體而言，新措施有助降低部分美國企業的進口成本，但美加貿易摩擦尚未完全
化解，後續仍須關注雙邊談判及關稅政策對供應鏈的影響。 
二、歐元區—法國動盪短期內難降溫，料將使央行保持審慎觀望態度 
 歐元區 10 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)下降 1.3 點至 51.9，抵銷上月的強勁
增幅，但為連續五個月高於 50 的榮枯線，主要係受到法國大規模抗議活動的影
響，使法國綜合 PMI 下降 2.7 點至 48.4；此外，德國綜合PMI 下降 1 點至 52.8，
亦反映近期通膨壓力增加 ， 使民眾傾向保守消費 。 若以產業區分 ， 歐元區服務業 、
製造業 PMI 分別降 1.3、0.5 點至 51.7、52.9；惟德國製造業 PMI 上升至相當強勁
的 54.2，反映德國政府擴張軍備投資以及基礎建設之需求，持續推動製造業生產
動能。

法國抗爭活動規模逐漸擴大 ， 各大工會與抗議團體已宣布在 10 月 31 日再度

善公共服務品質及凍結物價上漲。然而，在財政壓力巨大且國會缺乏穩定執政多
數的情況下，法國政府既無法強力通過重大的財政改革，也難以快速推出安撫反
對派的妥協法案或者公共支出。故部分較為悲觀的機構認為此波動盪可能會延續
至 2027 年 4 月的法國總統大選。受法國局勢影響，近期歐元區各國殖利率普遍
呈現上升趨勢，並對景氣產生暫時性的抑制效果。在此背景下，市場預期歐洲央
行在 10 月 28 日的政策會議將採取觀望立場，後續關注歐洲央行是否將透過政策
訊號或流動性工具，為平息法國主權債務利率而採取行動。 
三、日本—貿易逆差及通膨壓力延續，內需仍具韌性 
 9 月出口雖維持強勁 ， 受半導體製造設備及汽車出口增加所帶動 ， 出口額年增
16.7%；惟進口增幅更為顯著，9 月進口額年增 18.9%，其中原油及液化天然氣進
口額分別大增 51.1%及 39.2%，主要反映中東局勢推升能源價格，以及日本尋求
替代能源供應來源的影響。由於進口額高於出口額，9 月貿易逆差達 5,365 億日
圓，為連續第 5 個月呈現逆差；累計 2026 上半財年（4 至 9 月）貿易逆差亦較上
年同期擴大 98.8%至 2.82 兆日圓。整體而言，出口並未明顯轉弱，近期貿易逆差
擴大主要反映能源進口成本攀升，使進口增幅持續高於出口。 
 9 月全國消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.1%，即使政府透過能源及教育補貼等措
施抑制物價，通膨仍高於2%，顯示實際通膨動能更為強勁。從物價結構來看，石
油、原物料及工資成本上升已逐步傳導至終端價格，漲價範圍由家具、醫療用品
及休閒用品等商品 ， 進一步擴散至房屋維護 、 公共運輸 、 住宿及外食等服務項目 ，
顯示企業成本轉嫁範圍逐步擴大，通膨壓力亦由商品向服務領域擴散。隨通膨持
續高於 2%且漲價範圍擴大 ， 亦為日本央行進一步推動貨幣政策正常化提供支撐 。 
 10 月綜合 PMI 持平於 52.3，顯示經濟仍維持穩定擴張，其中製造業 PMI 下
降 0.8 點至 53.3，產出指數亦降至 3 月以來最低，主要反映新訂單及出口訂單成
長放緩，加上稀土金屬等原料供應受限，對生產形成一定壓力，惟生產與訂單仍
處擴張區間，尚未顯示製造業景氣明顯轉弱；服務業 PMI 回升 0.7 點至 52.0，新
業務及出口訂單同步改善，顯示服務需求仍具韌性，為支撐景氣的重要動能。另
一方面，能源、薪資及原物料成本居高不下，使投入價格壓力持續偏高，並逐漸

四、中國—第三季經濟成長率回升，但前景仍較為不利 
2026 年第三季 GDP 年增率初估值為 4.5%，較第二季的 4.3%上揚 0.2 個百分
點；累計 1-9 月 GDP 年增率則為 4.6%。第三季的二、三級產業年增率分別較第
二季上揚 0.4、0.1 個百分點至 3.4%， 其中製造業 、 批發零售業年增率分別達5.9%、
2.3%為主要動能 ， 抵銷建築業年減4.4%等拖累因素影響 。 整體而言 ， 第三季GDP
年增率上揚有部分受到中秋國慶連假落在 9 月末段(去年同期則於 10 月 1 日開
始)，致基期效果發揮部分影響，相關消費動能仍可能會在 10 月出現暫時性的回
落。 
在需求指標方面，(1)1-9 月累計零售額年增率為 1.1%，持平於 1-8 月的累計
年增率。其中 9 月零售額年增率增加 0.7 個百分點至 1.1%，部分受到新手機上市
帶動買氣，9 月通訊器材消費年增率高達 31.1%，加上中秋國慶長假帶動食品消
費年增率增加 1 個百分點至 2.1%所致 ；(2)1-9 月固定資產投資年減幅維持於 7.2%，
反映供應鏈移轉、政府主導去化過剩產能等因素導致民間投資需求急遽萎縮；萎
縮產業包含化工、醫藥、專用設備、汽車製造業、金屬製品業等；(3)1-9 月房地
產開發投資額年減幅由 19.9%擴大至 20.6%、施工面積年減幅為 12.8%，新建商
品房銷售面積年減幅擴大為 13.2%，房市投資、買氣皆持續低迷。 
受到出口維持高度成長帶動，9 月工業生產年增率較 8 月增加 0.3 個百分點
至 5.5%(累計 1-9 月工業生產年增率持平 1-8 月水準為 5.3%)。依產業別來看，9
月主要受到煤礦開採、化工、化纖等產業擴張影響，主因中秋與國慶長假前發電
業充實庫存水位，促使煤炭企業加快復產，加上油價上漲帶動相關中下游供應鏈
增產；惟高科技產品包含 IC、機器人、新能源車等年增率皆有放慢，反映去年中
秋國慶連假皆落在 10 月，使得去年 9 月工作日較多，致基期相對墊高的影響。 
展望未來 ， 儘管政府10 月初宣布釋放 5,500 億元人民幣的地方政府債務結存
限額，但額度下達到資金撥付往往需要數月時間，預期對於第四季的經濟影響有
限，使今年全年經濟成長率預估將落在 4.5%附近。此外，受歐盟右派勢力崛起及
保護主義影響，歐盟對中國貿易政策可能持續趨嚴，後續仍須關注雙方談判及溝
通結果，未來若外需動能減弱，預期中國政府將持續透過發展特定高科技產業及
扶植內需來支持經濟成長動能，以減輕房市不振及貿易環境惡化的衝擊。

五、印度及東南亞—新、馬出口保持強勁增速，菲律賓央行大舉升息 
(1)印度: 10 月 HSBC 綜合採購經理人指數（PMI）初值上升 0.8 點至 56.7，其中，
製造業 PMI 略下滑 0.2 點至 54.9、服務業 PMI 則上揚 1.0 點至 56.2，皆保持穩健
擴張態勢。由於美國、英國、中東及孟加拉等地的訂單增加，帶動新訂單及出口
訂單加速成長，製造商採購庫存增速創八個月來最快、成品庫存增速亦創歷史新
高，然而聘僱指數降至今年以來最低、未完成訂單下降，反映現階段人力充足。
另一方面，投入成本漲幅連續第七個月下降並降至近一年低點，產品售價漲幅亦
明顯放緩，通膨壓力緩解，有助提升企業獲利能力及消費者購買力，並且給予印
度央行（RBI）貨幣政策空間。 
(2)新加坡：9 月非石油國內出口（NODX）年增 68.4%，增速史上最快，連續第
23 個月正成長，其中，人工智慧需求強勁，帶動電子產品出口躍增 107.6%；非電
子產品出口亦大幅成長 54.2%，主要由船舶和遊艇組件所帶動。按出口目的地劃
分 ， 對香港出口激增 121.1%、 對台灣 、 韓國出口增幅達96%， 對美出口成長 73.2%，
但對印度出口下降 5.6%。展望未來，新加坡金融管理局在 10 月的宏觀經濟評估
報告中表示，直至 2027 年人工智慧 (AI) 驅動的成長將保持相對穩定， 支持未
來幾季經濟成長表現。由於基數較高以及人工智慧相關投資預計放緩，全球利率
上升可能會對新加坡主要貿易夥伴的消費和非人工智慧投資造成壓力，可能會影
響外部需求，薪資成長放緩、招聘謹慎以及運輸成本上升可能會在 2026 年下半
年對可自由支配支出和零售活動構成壓力，預計 2027 年 GDP 成長將放緩。 
(3)印尼：印尼央行 （BI）10 月會議連續第四次會議維持基準利率(7 天期逆回購利
率,BI-Rate)於 5.75%不變，貸款便利利率（LFR）維持在 6.50%不變，但將隔夜存
款便利利率（DFR）調高 25 個基點至 5%，旨在提高印尼盾資產的回報率，藉此
吸引並留住外資，同時宣布分兩階段調降存款準備率，11 月 1 日起下調 1 個百分
點至 8%，12 月 1 日起進一步降至 7%，以提供銀行體系流動性。整體而言，印尼
央行爲捍衛印尼盾，在 5 至 6 月間共升息 100 個基點，同時調降外資避險成本，
並採取「資金停留愈久、優惠愈高」的機制吸引外資流入。惟近期國際指數編纂
公司 MSCI 擬將印尼股市由”新興市場”降級為”獨立市場”，引發資金外流疑慮，
央行選擇利率按兵不動，搭配降準提供銀行體系流動性，以兼顧印尼盾穩定與國
內經濟需求，未來 MSCI 檢討進展(最快 2027 年 4 月公布)及印尼盾走勢，將持續
牽動央行後續政策。

用相關內容。

9

(4)菲律賓：菲律賓央行（BSP）10 月 22 日宣布大幅調升基準利率(逆回購利率)2
碼至 5.50%，為 2023 年以來最大幅度升息 。 央行指出 ， 9 月通膨率已升至 7.2%，
遠高於 2%-4%的目標區間；同時核心通膨持續攀升，顯示價格上漲已由食品與能
源擴散至整體經濟。BSP 亦擔憂中東情勢推升國際油價、運輸費率調漲，以及聖
嬰現象衝擊農業生產，進一步推高物價。此外，披索貶至歷史低點附近，增加進
口通膨風險，因此採取較激進的緊縮措施以穩定市場預期，貨幣政策重點已由支
持經濟成長轉向優先遏制通膨風險。 
(5)馬來西亞：9 月出口額年增 54.5%至 MYR2,151 億元，其中電子&電氣產品出
口大增 69.2%，持續反映 AI 帶動的強勁需求，礦產品出口亦大幅成長 56.3%，抵
銷農產品出口續跌 5.7%的影響，對台灣、美國出口大增 175.2%、89.9%、對新加
坡出口增幅亦達 50.4%。9 月進口額成長 52.7%至 MYR1,814 億元，受惠出口引
申需求，中間產品、資本貨物進口分別成長69.6%、20.9%，消費品進口轉為正成
長 18.2%，自中國和新加坡進口分別成長 67.6%、90.7%。進出口相抵，9 月貿易
順差達 MYR337 億元、年增 64.9%。 
六、台灣—外銷訂單創新高，產業景氣冷熱不均 
9 月外銷訂單達 1,142.1 億美元，再創單月新高，年增 62.4%，主要受惠於人
工智慧 、 高效能運算與雲端服務等應用蓬勃發展 ，以及消費性電子新品陸續推出 ，
帶動相關供應鏈維持高檔；加上國際原物料價格上漲，推升整體接單表現。惟各
產業接單動能仍呈分化，科技產品需求強勁，傳統產業景氣則相對疲弱。經濟部
指出，雖有年底節慶補貨潮挹注，但傳統產業部分產能過剩，終端需求仍未見明
顯回升。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD

12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持

## Document 106: 261027_gs_unimicron

Pages: 8

### Page 2

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology 
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT 
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We note (1) over 70% of ABF 
shipments are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and 
makes price hikes less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker 
than peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its 
ability to capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing 
more favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see a better pricing 
outlook to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF accounts for 10% 
of the company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers as the 
company is losing market share given its less favourable production yield and slow 
capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our outlook for 
limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally positive view in 
the long term on its profitability trajectory. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology 
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,270 for Unimicron is 
based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E 
(10.3x).  
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2) 
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster 
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

Hon Precision
Company description
Hon Precision is a leading Taiwanese manufacturer specializing in IC
semiconductor automation equipment. Headquartered in Taichung City,
Taiwan, it is most known globally for providing high-precision IC test
handlers, temperature control solutions, and complete automated integrated
solutions for major OSAT and IDM customers.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Hon Precision as we view it sitting in a structurally

protected position at the heart of the AI/HPC back-end test cycle, with
growth visibility extending well into the next few years — the company holds
>90% share of the AI/HPC FT handler market and 60-70% of the ASIC SLT
market. We forecast a solid strong sales revenue CAGR in 2025-28E with
gross margin continuing to expand. In our view, the binding constraint on Hon
Precision's near-term trajectory is now capacity, rather than demand —
placing the company in the early phase of a multi-year structural growth and

margin-expansion cycle.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$10,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 31x applied
to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates. Our PER target of 31x is set at
0.5-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC
testing handler market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR
of > 75% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-25. Our
estimated > 75% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average

(Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing equipment peers. As its peers
have been trading at  average  35x PER on 2027 /28 consensus EPS in the past
1 year, we believe Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target
PER.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price
volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we are not assigning a High
Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of > 75%, significantly stronger

than the growth outlook for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant
position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker capacity build for
semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI
projects; 3) slower-than-expected capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the

company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-
expected development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing
time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak adoption of high-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26E

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +39% QoQ, driven by: (1) AI data center: The 
next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp-up, with higher ASP and early 
delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first to deliver the latest model, around 2-3 
months ahead of peers, driving its Oct and onward revenue growth. Followed by 
GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks should fuel the growth 
in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27. As our Global Server TAM 
update shows, we expect the AI server rack global value TAM to grow at 163% CAGR in 
2025-28E to US$1tn, or 63% of the global server value TAM by 2028E. And (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlighted in September (report link), 
we reduced our global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m units to 1.1bn / 
1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we 
remain positive on the potential for global leading brands to outperform the market 
given their strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger 
consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium 
models to continue to gain market share, which should be positive to Hon Hai. Maintain 
Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect the company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +61% 
YoY/ +39% QoQ to NT$4.2 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 47%, 
or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both YoY and 
MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new smartphone 
models, and CPO switches. Management noted that AI-related products remain on track 
to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products continue to 
benefit from typical 2H seasonal tailwinds, which should support overall operational 
performance in line with current consensus expectations.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI servers ramp-up to drive growth ahead; model transition and PC 
shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26

Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entered quarter-end at a strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, riding 
on a reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramp up, along with 
growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better specification and high 
raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 4% QoQ, considering the 
high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp-up of new AI server racks. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA 
and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 2026E-28E along with the specification 
upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading US CSPs aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending.

Maintain Neutral on fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion

Wiwynn delivered strong Sep revenues, which were 19% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general server demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI server ramping up. We expect revenue YoY growth to remain strong at mid 
double-digit through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +60 YoY / +8% QoQ, 
driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM update), we raised AI servers implied AI chips shipment by 9% / 
19% in 2027 / 28E with ASIC penetration rate increasing to 58% by 2028E, and our US 
internet team forecasts that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will 
grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our US CSP capex 
estimates raised by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, supporting 
our positive view on Wiwynn. Maintain Buy with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$4,000

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 20.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.  
Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): AI server racks to see strong ramp-up with customer expansion and 
specification upgrades

Wistron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY to NT$381bn, or 18% lower than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the impact of shipment timing volatility in higher-ASP AI products. 
We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI 
server racks ramp-up in 4Q26, which should drive up the ASP , along with strong AI 
spending and expansion of the company’s customer base. The new GPU AI server racks 
have various racks across GPU, CPU, and LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming 
quarters. Sep PC shipment was up 7% MoM but down 32% YoY to 1.5m units, reflecting 
the slow PC demand amid the consumption pull-in under the high memory cost. Despite 
PC shipments growth likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in since late 
2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, driving 2H26 
revenue up +41% HoH. Maintain Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$291 (12.6x 2027E P/E 
vs. 30.4% NI YoY in 2027-28E).

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix upgrade

Pegatron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY / 143% MoM to NT$215bn, or 64% higher than 
our estimate. This reflects a strong ramp-up in AI servers in Sep. Sep PC shipments were

high memory costs. Prior to September, the company suffered from model transition (i.e. 
declining demand for older models while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up 
phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 4% / 6% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratio. We raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 16% / 28% / 27%, considering the AI server shipment ramp-up 
and higher ASP on better specification. Given the much lower GM of AI server racks 
compared to Pegatron’s other products, our changes in gross profits narrow to 9% / 12% 
/ 15% in 2026E-28E. The opex ratio for 2026-28E is cut by 0.1 ppt/ 0.2 ppt/ 0.1 ppt to 
reflect the improved operational efficiency.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

What surprised us 
AirTAC reported 3Q26 results today (October 29th). 3Q OP was in 
line with our estimates, but was 5% higher than BBG consensus. 
OPM was 33.9% in 3Q26, largely in line with our estimates; although 
GM of 49.4% was higher than our estimates by 0.9ppt, it was offset 
by higher opex (NT$1,778mn vs. GSe of NT$1,669mn). Total 
revenue was largely in line with GSe but was 4% above consensus, as 
the company enjoyed solid demand from the electronics industry 
(33% YoY in 3Q26 vs. 20% YoY in 2Q26, which accounted for 28% of 
total 3Q26 revenue). 
What surprised us the most during the results call was the 
company’s solid guidance on industry growth (mentioned low single 
to mid single digit % YoY growth is sustainable for the longer term 
for the China pneumatic market) and the company further 
mentioned that it could outgrow the market by 10ppt YoY in 2027. 
Moreover, the company expects all 
electronics/automotive/machine tools/general 
machinery/textile/packaging to see double digit YoY growth in both 
2026/27, thanks to the China government’s ongoing stimulus 
policies. Management reiterated 2026 revenue guidance in Rmb 
terms to reach 20%+ YoY growth (could reach higher YoY in NT$ 
terms), and maintained 2026 OPM guidance of 34%.  
The company reiterated its utilization rate target for its linear guide 
business to reach 50% by 2026 end (vs. 40%+ in 3Q26). The 
company continues to see peers raise prices for their linear guide 
products, and management thinks the company is well-positioned 
to gain market share with its stable pricing and shorter product lead 
time (less than a month vs. a key competitor at 4 months, see here).  
Going into 4Q26, we expect revenue to reach NT$11,425mn (-1% 
QoQ,+26% YoY), as we expect solid YoY across nearly all end 
markets. Rising raw material cost remains a key risk, while the

### Page 3

company continues to maintain a stable pricing strategy within the China market (which 
accounts for 90%+ of total revenue); thus we expect GM to slightly decline to 48.7% 
(-0.7ppt QoQ and +1.2ppt YoY). We continue to see the company’s ongoing effort in 
improving internal efficiency, which could partially offset the negative impact from rising 
material cost. 
We hold our conservative though optimistic view on the pneumatic industry outlook, as 
we continue to see downside risk in consumer electronics shipments (smartphone, etc) 
and the negative impact from rising raw material cost to persist over the coming 
quarters. We expect the company to continue to gain share in the China pneumatic 
market with its stable pricing strategy as well as its shift towards higher-quality 
pneumatic products.

Investment view 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC as we expect the company will continue to 
outperform the overall pneumatic market. We are positive on the company’s latest 
comment that the overall China pneumatic market YoY growth could be sustained at low 
single to mid single digit for a longer period, and management further mentioned that 
the company will outperform the market by 10ppt in 2027. This is mainly driven by the 
company’s latest guidance that nearly all end markets could see double digit YoY growth 
in 2027. While the company did mention the risk of decelerating YoY growth for the 
battery industry (which has seen solid YoY growth over the past two years), we expect 
solid growth from smaller segments, including machine tool/general 
machinery/textile/packaging, could offset the risk (we expect the 4 segments to account 
for 24% of total 2027E revenue vs. battery at 13%). 
We believe AirTAC is well positioned to overcome potential industry demand risks with 
its strong B/S and healthy cash flow position, which could further support market share 
gains during industry downturns (historically, AirTAC has accelerated its market share 
gains during industry downcycles, supported by its solid B/S and cash flow 
management). We also expect the company’s ROE to continue to improve in the coming 
years (ROE should improve to 26.2% in 2028E per GSe vs. 16%-17% in 2022-25) 
supporting a high dividend payout ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe). 
As a result, we maintain our Buy rating on AirTAC with unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,895.

### Page 4

Earnings and TP revisions 
We revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 1/1% to factor in the 
better-than-expected growth in the China pneumatic market. We revise up our 2026E 
OPM by 0.3ppt to factor in actual 3Q26 results and revise up our 2027/28E OPM by 
0.3/0.1ppt as we continue to expect the company to focus on enhancing internal 
efficiency. We revise down our 2026E EPS estimates by 4% after factoring in a higher tax 
rate in 2H26. 
Also, we introduce our 2029E earnings estimates in this report. 
Maintain our Buy rating and 12m TP of NT$1,895, which is based on unchanged P/E of 
26x on our 2027E EPS, as we believe the company could trade at 0.25x STDV above the 
average PE multiple during the previous pneumatic upcycle.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis - AirTAC
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC

continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring.

Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC.
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,895 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on

our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle).
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in
mainland China.

公司研究報告

1 
6770 力積電(Powerchip)

報告日期： 2026/10/22 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 在記憶體及邏輯產品價格季增 20% 的帶動下，使整體毛
利率跳升 19 個百分點 ， 來到47.69% ，單 季 稅 後EPS 1.38 元，
優於預期。  
 4Q26 將反映晶圓投片及產出的時間差，使出貨量及 ASP 將再
增加，帶動營收、毛利率雙位升。  
 AI 帶動矽電容需求大爆發，目前力積電產能僅 1 萬片 /月，但
2027 年需求將達 3～5 萬片 / 月。

一. 反映投片與產出落差， 4Q26 營收、毛利率雙升  
力積電 3Q26 晶圓出貨量 41.6 萬片， QoQ-1.44% ，主因為銅鑼廠
機器搬遷所致，且 3D AI Foundry 營收占比也因遷廠而由 2Q26
的 5.4% 下滑到 1.4%， 但因記憶體及邏輯產品價格持續上漲 ，ASP
季增 20% ，使力積電 3Q26 整體營收達 212.78 億元，
QoQ+23.07% ，YoY+79.71% ，在價格上漲及產能利用率提升的帶
動下，整體毛利率由 2Q26 的 28.28% 大幅上升至 47.69% ，稅後
EPS 1.38 元，優於預期。

由於力積電投片與產出的落差 30%( 邏輯遞延 2～3 個月， DRAM
遞延 4 個月 )，因此， 4Q26 的晶圓出貨及 ASP 將比 3Q26 更高，
加上 ， 銅鑼廠的機器設備已完成搬遷到新竹廠 ，Interposer 及 WoW
的產能將在 11～12 月逐步恢復生產，其中 Interposer 將於 2027
年達到滿載。綜上，我們預估力積電 4Q26 營收 234.3 億元，
QoQ+10.11% ，YoY+87.51% ，毛利率進一步提升至 51.81% ，單
季稅後 EPS 1.64 元，合計 2026 年營收 755.71 億元，
YoY+61.72% ，在記憶體價格大漲的帶動下，毛利率由 2025 年的
-3.29% 跳升至 37.8% ，稅後 EPS 6.58 元(含售廠給美光 )。

二. 2027 年矽電容需求大爆發  
力積電營運轉型將陸續展現成果，美光授權生產的 DRAM 的 1P
製程，將於 2026 完成 PDK ，2Q27 試產， 2028 年有機會貢獻全
年營收； HBM 的後段製程代工 PWF ，由美光提供設備， 2026 年
試產， 4Q27 量產， 2028 年貢獻全年營收； 3D AI Foundry 方面，
堆疊技術將由 Micro bump 進入到 Hybrid bonding ，以及
Interposer 的放量生產 ， 在外溢效果持續下 ，2027 年產能將滿載；
Wafer on Wafer 方面，在銅鑼廠設千完全搬遷至新竹廠後，目前
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：95 元 
     11 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：66.2 元 
     7.67 X 2027 EPS 
     2026/10/21 收盤

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

2 
已小量試產， 1H27 將正式量產。由於建廠及買購設備需大額資本
支出，風險相對較高，因此， PWF 由美光提供設備，未來的 WoW
也將會採取輕資產的模式，降低營運風險，不過，因大廠均將產
能移去生產高階 DRAM ，38nm 、25nm 、20nm 這些傳統 DRAM
製程未來全球將只剩力積電生產，預估此一需求將長達 10～15
年。

此外 ， 因AI 晶片的高功耗及高速傳輸需求 ， 為提升電源傳輸效率 、
降低電阻、去雜訊等問題，將帶動 IPD 需求大爆發，力積電目前
以現有 DRAM 的 38nm、25nm 製程做生產 IPD，恰好目前 DRAM
產能供不應求的情況下 ，IPD 的產能只能配置約 1 萬片 /月，而2027
年全球 IPD 的需求將達 3～5 萬片 /月，且能見度已延伸至 2028
年，顯見需求遠超過力積電所能提供，因此，力積電已在尋找建
廠用地，且將聯合策略客戶共同投資新產能。

三. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 8.63 元 
AI 進入到推理後對記憶體已需求量更甚以往，但因全球記憶體產
能嚴重供不應求的情況下， GUP 大廠將 12 層 HBM 降低到 8 層，
顯見記憶體緊俏情況嚴峻，也使記憶體價格易漲難跌，預估 2027
年記憶體價格仍至少維持在高點，加上成熟製程處在有利的價格
環境， 2026 年漲價雙位數後， 2027 年將再漲 10～20% ，因此，
我們預估力積電 2027 年營收 1223.38 億元， YoY+61.89% ，毛利
率 49.89% ，稅後 EPS 8.63 元。

四. 投資建議為買進，目標價 95 元 
受惠 AI 帶動的記憶體漲價潮持續， 2026 年力積電將呈現轉虧為
盈，且 2026 及 2027 年獲利都將大幅成長，加上銅鑼廠售予美光
且變成長期的代工合作夥伴關係，有利力積電長期營運穩定性，
也解決力積電資產負債表壓力，因此，投資建議為買進，目標價
95 元(11X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
力晶集團 05/2019 完成企業重組 ， 由旗下的力晶科技3 座 12 吋晶
圓廠及相關營業、資產，讓與力晶積成電子製造股份有限公司 (簡
稱“力積電” (6770)) 。力積電為聚焦專業晶圓代工、產業定位明
確，目前擁有 2 座 8 吋及 3 座 12 吋晶圓廠，將針對先進記憶體、
客製化邏輯積體電路與分離式元件的三大晶圓代工服務主軸，持
續 Open Foundry 營運模式，從晶片設計、製造服務，到設備、

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3 
產能分享，根據不同客戶的屬性和需求，共同建立緊密、彈性的
合作機制，且著眼半導體 3D 堆疊產業大趨勢，力積電與國際知名
大客戶密切配合開發 3D 堆疊封裝，將成為公司長線另一成長動
能。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1、佈局 3D IC 為公司未來運重要利基 
力積電有部份產品屬於記憶體產品，營運雖然將隨著市場波動，
受產業景氣循環大，然力積電善用其技術優勢，將其記憶體產品
與國際知名大廠合作，延伸至 3D 堆疊封裝技術，僅需利用舊製程
即可將晶片效能數倍提升，其以 55nm 的邏輯 IC 堆疊 38nm 的
DRAM 製程，封裝後的晶片用於挖礦機，效能較現有的解決方案
大幅提升 9 倍。由 於 透 過3D 堆疊可使晶片設計效率提高， 提升晶
片整體效能，減少晶片尺寸，加快晶片內部元件溝通速度，為半
導體先進封裝未來重要發展趨勢，且因先進封裝技術成本不斷提
升，客戶為考量使用的成本效益，未來將有更多廠商使用 3D 堆疊
的解決方案取代先進製程技術 ， 使得3D 堆疊技術長線發展具極大
潛力，力積電長線著眼於此一產業大趨勢，將積極深化此一佈局，
為公司長線營運提供良好動能。

2、四大營運方向引領公司未來成長動能 
力積電在經過 2019 年 3 座 8 吋廠及 2 座 12 吋廠的整併，加上邏
輯技術逐漸步上軌道後，使其在 40nm ～0.11um 的技術於全球站
穩腳步，具有指標地位。公司除將以此為基礎拓展多元應用，在
新技術方面亦不斷投入研發，展望未來，擬定四大方向引領公司
成長動能： 1、發展更先進的技術 --- 第四代半導體氧化銦鎵鋅
(IGZO) ，因 IGZO 的材料特性，其超低功耗的性能，僅為目前耗
電的 1/20， 及顯示器解析度能超過5,000DDIP ， 有助應用於AR/VR
的裝置有較佳續航力及超高解析度，使用者將完全沈浸於元宇宙
世界。 2、3D-Interchip 堆疊技術：與晶圓代工大廠台積電合作，
將記憶體與邏輯 IC 進行 3D 堆疊 ， 使晶片具更小體積 、 更低功耗，
更低成本，及開發更有效率。 3D 堆疊的 Wafer on Wafer 目前以
供應挖礦機需求為主，未來將專注於 AI 龐大需求。 3、Interchip ：
將邏輯元件直接放入記憶體元件，以提高記憶體效能，適用於
IoT 、AI 及邊緣運算。 4、車用電子晶片：因應節能減碳的趨勢，
電動車 / 新能源車將快速發展，公司將致力推進第三代半導體
GaN、SiC 技術製程，應用於 PMIC 。由於 PMIC 為車汽應用的重
要零組件之一，且 PMIC 的製造非用最先進的半導體製程，力積
電目前的製程範圍恰好符合最佳生產製程的節點，而電動車的興
起將帶動 PMIC 的應用大幅成長，加上 AI 趨勢確立下，對 PMIC
有更高需求，為公司未來重要佈局方向及營運重要成長動能。

## Document 205: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

## Document 207: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-29
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 256: Anthropic招股書裡最值得看的14個細節

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic招股書裡最值得看的14個細節.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

以上資料為元大證券國際金融業務部依上手提供研究報告摘譯，僅供內部教育訓練使用。 
Anthropic 招股書裡最值得看的 14 個細節 
2026/10/27

摘要： 
⚫ 華爾街見聞整理了人工智慧獨角獸 Anthropic 提交 S-1 招股書的核心數據與關鍵細節。公司
擬以代碼「ANT」登陸那斯達克，目標估值介於 1.5 兆至 2 兆美元之間。 
⚫ 營收五個季度漲了約 30 倍，第二季已經轉盈，上半年帳面卻虧了 807 億美元，算力帳單還簽
到了 2045 年……Anthropic 招股書裡最值得看的 14 個細節，不少都帶著這樣的反差。更少見
的是控制權：普通股東幾乎沒有話語權，50.1%的投票權握在七位創辦人手中。 
1. 瘋狂的營收增速與放緩跡象  
⚫ 營收從 2023 年的 4,100 萬美元飆升至 2025 年的 45.85 億美元，2026 上半年達 
162.86 億美元；第二季單季營收 115.55 億美元，五個季度內暴增約 30 倍。今年前 9 
個月累計收入突破 330 億美元，過去一年人均創收達 660 萬美元。不過第二季至第三季
營收季增幅已放緩至接近 50%，公司警示早期爆發增長後未來可能停滯。

### Page 2

以上資料為元大證券國際金融業務部依上手提供研究報告摘譯，僅供內部教育訓練使用。 
2. 半年帳面虧損 807 億美元，主因為公允價值變動  
⚫ 2026 上半年帳面淨虧損達 807.48 億美元，但其中約 723.88 億美元是因公司估值大幅
上升，導致可轉換票據、衍生負債與 SAFE 評估價值提高所認列的帳面虧損，並無現金流
出。扣除股權激勵等非現金費用後，上半年調整後營業利潤為 16.18 億美元，調整後淨利
潤達 26.71 億美元。 
3. 算力成本佔比驟降，經營利潤成功轉正  
⚫ 每 1 美元營收對應的算力與基礎設施支出，從 2025 年第一季的超過 2.5 美元降至 
2026 年第二季的不足 50 美分（算力支出佔比由 2023 年的 10 倍以上降至 2026 上
半年的 55%）。2026 年第二季經營利潤達 9.74 億美元（利潤率 8%），上半年營運現
金流淨流入 39 億美元，主要受惠於「閉環研發」模型訓練效率提升、推理優化、跨晶片
自建調度及降價策略。

4. 客戶結構多元化，頭部集中度下降  
⚫ 今年前 9 個月前五大客戶貢獻 25% 營收，但佔比從第一季的約 36% 降至第三季的 
21%，已無單一客戶佔比超過 10%。年消費破 10 萬美元的客戶超過 11,900 家，超過
半數名列財富 500 強（但其支出佔自身總預算不到 0.1%）。淨收入留存率（不含訂閱）
高達約 900%，且第三季有超過 80% 的營收來自近 6 個月內發布的新模型。

### Page 3

以上資料為元大證券國際金融業務部依上手提供研究報告摘譯，僅供內部教育訓練使用。

5. 5%至 7%的收入來自違規客戶  
⚫ 前 9 個月約有 5% 至 7%（約 16 億至 23 億美元）的營收來自事後被判定違反使用規
範或商業條款的客戶，主因是監控、調查至封號存在時間差。隨著 8、9 月加大封禁力
度，此類收入佔比將下降，可能對未來的營收與增速造成一定拉低。 
6. 算力長約承諾超 8,000 億美元，最遠鎖定至 2045 年  
⚫ 各項固定或保底採購、已簽約未起算之租約及後續長期合約合計超過 8,300 億美元，包括
博通（五年 1,610 億美元租約及後續最高 1,500 億美元支出）、AMD（逾 200 億美
元）、三大雲端巨頭（十年至少 2,694 億美元）等。合約多採能源長約常見的「照付不
議」（用量不足仍須補足差額）條款，成本提前鎖定，而公司 80% 收入卻採依用量實際
結算，形成節奏差異。

### Page 4

以上資料為元大證券國際金融業務部依上手提供研究報告摘譯，僅供內部教育訓練使用。
7. 每月支付給馬斯克的 SpaceX 算力費高達 31 億美元
⚫ Anthropic 租用 SpaceX.AI（原 xAI）位於孟菲斯的資料中心，合約總額最高達 1,205
億美元，合約至 2029 年。12 月算力全面上線後每月費用約 31 億美元，該月費已接近
SpaceX 今年首季整個 AI 業務營收（8.18 億美元）的四倍之多。
8. 巨頭持股版圖與極短的解禁安排
⚫ 若以 2 兆美元估值計算，亞馬遜全面轉股後約持股近 20%（約 4,000 億美元，超出
12% 上限部分暫以票據保留），Google 持股約 7% 並擁有後續增資與基石投資權限。
解禁期不同於傳統 180 天限制，上市後 76 天內設五個解禁日，首日即允許非創辦人董
事、高管與部分員工減持套現。
9. 特殊雙層治理架構，創辦團隊握有 50.1%絕對投票權
⚫ 普通股東對決策幾乎無控制權，亞馬遜與 Google 持有的 N 類股亦無投票權。公司頂層

由「長期利益信託」（LTBT）掌控制衡，而七位共同創辦人則透過僅 1 股、每人出資 1
美元認購的「F 類股」掌握 50.1% 的絕對投票權，屬於那斯達克規則下的「受控公
司」。
10. 股價每漲 50%，最高提撥 2%股權投入公益
⚫ 上市前已將全面攤薄股本的 5%（約 8.47 億股 N 類股）劃入 Anthropic 公益基金。董
事會批准未來股價若從發行價起每上漲 50%，可再額外注入最高 2% 股份；同時七位共
同創辦人承諾將個人 80% 的股權收益用於公益捐贈。
11. 長達 90 多頁的「生存級」風險因子揭露
⚫ 篇幅約為 SpaceX 的三倍，直接提及技術可能對人類構成「災難性或生存級風險」、可能
被惡意行為者用於開發致命武器，甚至可能衍生出模型抵制關機、操縱資訊或勒索等失控
行為。招股書直言，此類系統性風險恐怕連整個全球保險業都無力承保。
12. 創辦團隊向投資者發出的理念警告
⚫ 七位創辦人公開呼籲投資人切勿純以短期財務交易視角看待公司，直言部分商業決策會在
公共利益與利潤最大化之間權衡，作法可能與股東預期背道而馳。同時，團隊也著重強調

創始成員至今未流失，與競爭對手 OpenAI 形成對比。
13. 上市股票代碼為「ANT」，展現低自我企業文化
⚫ Anthropic 將以股票代碼「ANT」在那斯達克掛牌。招股書以「low-ego」（低自我）形
容內部文化，員工內部自稱為「Ants」（螞蟻），形成文化與代碼的巧妙呼應。

### Page 5

以上資料為元大證券國際金融業務部依上手提供研究報告摘譯，僅供內部教育訓練使用。 
14. 市場下一步檢驗指標：10 月 ARR 表現  
⚫ 市場高度關注 S-1 補充文件中將揭露的 10 月 Run-Rate Revenue（以近 28 天用量年
化加計訂閱費）。目前市場預期 730 億美元為合理基準，若突破 780 億美元，將大幅提
高市場對其在 12 月達成 1,000 億美元 ARR 的信心，成為左右其 1.5 兆至 2 兆美元

## Document 302: Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13023135     1

Economy 
Entering the age of the older consumer 
01 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• The older consumer is moving center stage. America's population has been aging for decades, but the shift has accelerated: the 
share aged over 60 increased by almost 10 percentage points (pp) between 1995 and 2025. And the Census Bureau projects the 
share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population. 
• More free time means a different spending mix. People aged 65 and over spend around two and a half fewer hours per day 
working and roughly two hours more on leisure than the overall adult population. Bank of America data shows that older 
households allocate relatively more of their card spending to groceries and travel, and less to restaurants, gasoline, general 
merchandise and clothing. 
• Households aged over 55 held close to $140 trillion in net worth in the second quarter of 2026, giving many older consumers 
considerable capacity to spend on travel and other leisure categories. But wealth is not evenly distributed, and healthcare and 
other cost pressures remain significant for older households on tighter or fixed incomes.

Population aging is nothing new, but it is accelerating  
The US population has been growing relatively “older” for a long time. Exhibit 1 shows that the proportion of the population 
under 20 years old shrank from the mid-1960s through 2025. At the same time, the proportion of the population in older age 
groups has been rising.  
Exhibit 1: Over time the US population has steadily aged

Additionally, there has been a notable acceleration in the relative aging of the population in recent years. For example, between 
1995 and 2025, the proportion of the population aged over 60 rose by almost 10 percentage points (pp) to 25%.  
Several factors have driven this. First, fertility rates in the US, as in much of the world, have fallen sharply. This has slowed 
natural population growth and, by reducing the number of younger people relative to older cohorts, has increased the share of 
the population at older ages. Second, more recently, net inward migration to the US has slowed. Because migrants tend to be 
younger than the overall population, weaker immigration also tends to raise the average age. Finally, rising life expectancy over 
much of the post-war period has allowed more people to survive into older age, further increasing the older population's share.  
Exhibit 2: The trend towards an older composition of the population is expected to continue  
Forecast changes in the share of population by age group relative to 2025 (pp)

Source: United States Census Bureau 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Lower fertility and, more recently, weaker net migration look set to slow overall US population growth in the decades ahead. As a 
result, older Americans are likely to play an increasingly important role in the economy, both as a share of the population and, in 
many age groups, in absolute numbers. The Census Bureau projects the share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 
2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population (Exhibit 2). For businesses, understanding how the needs and spending patterns 
of older consumers differ from those of younger generations will therefore become increasingly relevant. 
Older age groups allocate their time differently  
Why does this matter? One reason is that older people tend to spend their time and money quite differently from younger adults. 
Most obviously, many work less or have retired altogether. Data from the American Time Use Survey (ATUS) shows that people 
aged 65 and over spend, on average, around two and a half fewer hours per day working than the population aged 15+ (Exhibit 
3). 
That leaves considerably more time for other activities. People aged 65 and over spend around two hours more per day on 
leisure and sports activities, with television viewing accounting for a particularly large share of this additional leisure time 
(Exhibit 4).

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Older households also spend differently  
Bank of America internal data on credit and debit card spending provides insights into how these differences in older 
generations’ use of time also impact their spending. Rather than splitting our spending data by generation, as we often do, here 
we split by age bands, allowing for consistent comparisons over time.  
Exhibit 5 shows how the composition of card spending of households aged 61-75 and those older than 75 differs from that of all 
households. A few things stand out. For one, older households devote a greater share of their overall card spending to groceries 
(food and beverages). Households aged 61-75 also allocate more spending to travel (airlines and lodging). And restaurants and 
bars, gasoline, general merchandise and clothing account for a smaller share of older households’ card spending.  
These differences may seem fairly intuitive. Older households have more leisure hours, so spend relatively more on travel to 
enjoy them. Previously we found that more available time may support travel particularly around retirement, although the 
discretionary spending lift can be temporary (for more, read our publication: Paychecks to pensions: The evolution of retiree 
spending).  
Older households are also likely to have less need to update their work wardrobes, so they probably spend less on clothing.

Still, older households’ relatively lower spending on restaurants and bars is a little more curious, perhaps, given dining out could 
be one way of making use of more leisure time. Indeed, when we look at how these spending shares are evolving, we find a 
nuanced picture. While households aged 61-75 do spend relatively less on restaurants and bars than the overall population, they 
appear to be narrowing this gap. But for those older than 75 this is not the case. In our view, the pandemic may still be weighing 
on dining-out spending among this older age group (Exhibit 6).

Aging presents opportunities, but also challenges 
In our view, the aging of the US population presents important opportunities for the economy and businesses. Older consumers 
have more leisure time and businesses that cater to these demands may therefore benefit. 
Moreover, some older consumers hold considerable amounts of net wealth and have been some of the biggest beneficiaries of 
recent rises in equities. Using data from the Federal Reserve, Exhibit 8 shows that as of the second quarter of 2026 the net 
worth of those households aged 55 and over was close to $140 trillion, around three-quarters of the total. And with equity 
markets rising strongly, these age groups have seen a more than 20% increase in their net worth over the past two years, 
potentially enhancing their spending power further.  
But an important caveat is that not all older consumers are in this position. Previous Institute work found evidence consistent 
with equity-market wealth effects being concentrated toward higher-income households (read our publication: Paychecks to 
pensions: The evolution of retiree spending). And Census Bureau research has found that around 14% of Social Security 
recipients aged over 65 rely on it for over 90% of their income. For older households without a wealth cushion, recent elevated 
cost pressures, like gasoline, will likely have put them under more significant financial strain. They will also be more dependent 
on cost-of-living (COLA) increases in Social Security to underpin their spending.  
And, more broadly, outside of card spending, healthcare costs are a much larger factor in older households’ budgets (Exhibit 9). 
The evolution of these costs going forward will be a key variable for older consumers’ budgets.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts.  
Durables spending is defined as spending on electronics, building materials, auto-related spending and furniture. Premium durables spending is 
based on a selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively higher value products. Conversely, value durables spending is based on a 
selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively lower value products.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

### Page 7

01 October 2026    7

## Document 333: Is AI Useless_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Is AI Useless_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Is AI Useless?
Date:    15 minutes ago
Is AI Useless?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Is AI Useless?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 26 Oct 2026
View in browser

I. AI's Toxic Financial Trifecta
Recent AI jitters have been fueled by a potentially toxic trifecta of financial
constraints: elevated borrowing costs, uncertain investment returns, and
concerns about whether sufficient capital exists to finance everyone's
ambitious AI spending plans. Consider the following:
(1) Elevated borrowing costs. The 10-year Treasury yield is hovering
near the upper end of its "old normal" range of 4.0%-5.0% (chart). That
puts it at the highest level since 2007. Overall, credit spreads (the extra

### Page 2

yield paid over equivalent US Treasuries, such as the 10-year note) have
not blown out uniformly across the board. Instead, the market has
bifurcated sharply between the pristine balance sheets of mega-cap tech
companies and the leveraged balance sheets of pure-play
infrastructure/data center borrowers.
Yields on AI debt have not broadly surged relative to Treasuries for the
dominant Big Tech issuers, whose balance sheets continue to be treated
virtually like "proxy sovereigns." However, the credit market is increasingly

imposing credit discipline, demanding significantly higher risk premiums
and spreads from the leveraged second- and third-tier infrastructure
borrowers that are building out the physical plumbing of AI. Increased
concerns about the credit quality of AI borrowers have been reflected in
widening credit default spreads (chart).

### Page 3

(2) Uncertain returns. Gartner estimates that AI data center operators
must generate $8.5 trillion in cumulative revenues through 2030 to cover
their investments and associated financing costs and still achieve a 12%
return on invested capital. Yet Gartner projects no more than $3.6 trillion in
attributable AI revenues over that period. In our view, if the numbers don't
add up, the pace of the AI buildout will slow. That wouldn’t be a calamity.
(3) Increasingly constrained capital. Meta's Q3 earnings report

intensified concerns about capital availability. Despite strong revenue
growth, the company reported negative free cash flow for the first time
since 2012 and announced new non-voting shares to facilitate fundraising,
warning of an "increasingly difficult fundraising environment." Today, Meta
unveiled plans to raise up to $95 billion through equity offerings.
Meanwhile, Broadcom CEO Hock Tan identified capital as potentially the
biggest constraint on AI development. BlackRock CEO Larry Fink

described competition for capital as the fiercest of his career, while Nvidia
CEO Jensen Huang observed that neocloud demand is "off the charts,"
but capital is scarce.
Today, Elon Musk warned that more than 200 specialized AI cloud
providers (i.e., neoclouds) are headed for a wave of "guppy-level death
and consolidation." Musk stated that debt markets have "almost entirely
shut off access to money for any company that doesn't have substantial
revenue independent of AI data centers." In recent weeks, the neocloud

providers’ stock prices have tumbled (chart).

### Page 4

On balance, hyperscalers’ stock prices have held up relatively well (chart).
II. Will AI Pay Off?
Demand for compute nevertheless remains extraordinary. Global
spending on cloud infrastructure services surged 43% y/y to $143 billion in
Q2, its fastest growth in eight years. But how sustainable is that demand
growth? Ultimately, the marginal economic benefits derived from AI must
justify the marginal cost of supplying the necessary compute.
We believe AI is already generating tangible economic benefits, even if

they have yet to become clearly visible in aggregate macroeconomic
statistics. Consider the following:
(1) Anthropic's S-1. Anthropic’s IPO filing today offered five revealing
takeaways: First, revenues soared from $1.2 billion in H1-2025 to $16.3
billion in H1-2026. Second, operating losses narrowed from $2.1 billion to
$693 million, with Q2 marking the company's first quarterly operating profit
of $974 million. Third, revenue per dollar of compute expense surged 5.2-

fold to $2.08 between Q1-2025 and Q2-2026. Fourth, Anthropic held
$131.7 billion in cash and short-term investments as of June, alongside
$3.9 billion in H1 operating cash flow. Fifth, its enterprise footprint
expanded rapidly, with more than 11,900 customers spending at least
$100,000 annually as of September, up from roughly 950 a year earlier.
(2) Corporate America's emerging AI payoffs. An August Janus
Henderson study of 109 companies found that 69% reported some margin

### Page 5

improvement associated with productivity gains and AI-related cost
reductions. Among the examples cited, Shell reported $400 million in
annual savings and a 45% reduction in unplanned downtime; Freeport-
McMoRan increased mill throughput 10% while cutting unit costs by up to
15%; inventories at one Hitachi facility fell 50% and lead times shortened
by 77%; and IBM reported $4.5 billion in AI-related savings in 2025.
Last Thursday, Barclays announced plans to eliminate approximately

5,000 jobs, or 5% of its workforce, as AI and broader technological
modernization improve efficiency. PayPal is targeting $1.5 billion in
annualized savings, partly through AI adoption.
(3) Corporate profit margins. Corporate America is becoming more
profitable. The forward profit margin of the S&P 500 excluding its
Information Technology sector has been climbing this year, reaching
12.9% in October (chart). Excluding just the Semiconductors industry, the

forward profit margin rose to a record 14.4%. The forward profit margin in
Semiconductors rose to a record 51.8% in the week of October 23. But
most of the industry’s margin expansion since 2024 is attributable to
capacity constraints pushing prices higher.
A closer look reveals encouraging trends in industries where AI
applications could be particularly beneficial. Forward profit margins have
expanded in numerous financial industries where AI offers significant

opportunities for automation (chart).

### Page 6

Health Care stands to benefit from AI applications that can streamline
clinical workflows and administrative processes (chart).
(4) Bottom line. We aren't yet seeing significant AI-driven profit margin
expansion across the economy, but anecdotal evidence of AI-related
productivity gains is mounting. We expect AI adoption to become
increasingly widespread across industries, eventually delivering
substantial productivity growth and boosting corporate profit margins. AI's

economic payoff may not yet be obvious in aggregate statistics, but that
doesn't mean it isn't already materializing.
💡
Join the discussion with Dr. Ed below! Paid members can now
leave comments or questions at the end of our QuickTakes, and it

### Page 7

clause.

## Document 353: MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 ～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCIは改革の進捗を注視～
